HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0cd744d50d9ba60adc9f","text":"1. **Night as an arriving cover, not a static object.** `ٱلَّيْل` anchors `ل ي ل:B001` (night and its darkness), and `يَغْشَىٰ` anchors both `غ ش و:B001` (a covering veil) and `غ ش و:B003` (coming upon or visiting). The temporal `إِذَا`, followed by day’s matching manifestation in 92:2, gives the pair an eventive reversal: cover comes upon the field, then disclosure comes upon it. The packet supplies succession and lexical branches; the inference is that the shared event structure makes covering an active arrival. The ayah thus reads as a recurring takeover of visibility, not merely “night is dark.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B002","root_001088/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_29976f0d574f4cf1c716","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:1:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki inkâr, ateş ve yüz çevirme sahnesi, gece örtüsünü bilinci kaplayan ve kişiyi kuşatan bir sıkıntının ön görünümü hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B001","root_001088/B003","root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3d5cf265234c993d7d31","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:1:1\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:1:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gece yalnızca karanlık bir durum değil, görüş alanına gelip onu etkin biçimde devralan bir örtü olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_590e6762f977dfd9b690","text":"92:13 and 92:20 filled out the guidance: God owns first/last and gives the face-direction by which the path is read. The gift/offering branch of `ه د ي` became stronger after the later giver’s wealth was purified by being sent without human repayment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_815938b07dc9693ad543","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:1:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gece örtüsü, sonradan gündüz ve karşıt yollarla açığa çıkacak insan çabasının henüz görünmeyen evresine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bc94369a13def1b78161","text":"2. **Night as overwhelming affliction and covered consciousness.** Evidence: `غ ش و` B002 (overwhelming event/affliction), B005 (consciousness covered over), activated by 92:14-16 fire and denial/turning away. Structural cue: the first oath uses a verb that can cover persons, not just landscape. Mechanism: moral denial behaves like a night that overtakes perception before the fire overtakes the person. Abductive move: I infer analogy from physical covering to cognitive/eschatological covering. Reading change: “when it covers” can foreshadow being engulfed by one’s own refusal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bcf1c63ba76c8f37af83","text":"1. **Night as covering veil over moral visibility.** Evidence: `ل ي ل` B001 (night/darkness), `غ ش و` B001 (covering veil). Structural cue: night is immediately reversed by day’s disclosure in 92:2 and later by the explicit exposure of two strivings in 92:4-10. Mechanism: covering delays recognition, while subsequent ayat expose what the cover hid. Abductive move: I add the directional claim that concealment makes the later disclosure meaningful. Reading change: the oath is not only cosmic; it models the hidden phase of human striving.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c03a66395bf45a1e47bf","text":"92:2 first corrected any reading of the cover as final by giving it an exact disclosure-counterpart. More surprisingly, 92:3 activated `غ ش و:B004`; the generative analogy remains subordinate to night’s grammatical covering, but it makes concealment potentially productive rather than simply privative.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eed28fc043a014f008f9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:1:3\",\"root_001088\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Erkek ile dişinin yaratılışı, gecenin örtmesini gizli bir üretken birleşmeye benzetmeye imkân verir; ancak bu benzetme ayetin doğrudan gece anlatımının yerini tutmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fc400317d85221f85132","text":"2. **An exploratory generative covering analogy.** `يَغْشَىٰ` remains grammatically predicated of night, but `غ ش و:B004` preserves the image of sexual covering. The immediately following creation of male and female in 92:3 activates that dormant image, while the fixed night-cover remains the controlling construction. The abductive move is analogical, not a change of subject: cosmic covering is read against embodied coupling as a hidden generative relation. This tentatively changes the night from pure concealment into a cover under which differentiation and generation become thinkable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
